    "originalFile": "\\begin{abstract}\nWe study structural degeneration in neurosymbolic reasoning trained with GRPO: models achieve high prove rates while most outputs embed the conclusion among premises---a degenerate shortcut invisible to execution reward.\nWe introduce \\emph{Solver Trace Ablation} (STA), a diagnostic applying leave-one-out necessity testing to solver inputs; the fraction of necessary components yields the \\emph{Intensional Structural Reward} (ISR), a component-redundancy indicator whose diagnostic value lies in the mode-level breakdown it enables.\nAs a diagnostic, component-necessity testing reveals a collapse trajectory hidden from prove rate: ISR falls to near-zero over training while prove rate rises, with each seed converging to a qualitatively different hacking mode.\nA supervised fine-tuning baseline corroborates that the degradation is GRPO-specific.\nWhen repurposed as a training reward, ISR exhibits a compositional Goodhart effect: models shift from conclusion embedding to tautological shortcutting without reducing overall hacking prevalence ($p{=}0.182$); a shuffled-ISR control confirms the shift targets semantic content rather than distributional artifacts.\nCross-logic (Prolog) and cross-dataset (ProofFOL) evidence confirms structural degeneration generalizes, documenting ISR's core asymmetry: reliable as a diagnostic, dataset-dependent as an optimization target.\\footnote{Code and data: \\url{[anonymized]}}\n\\end{abstract}\n",

          "+We introduce \\emph{Solver Trace Ablation} (STA), a diagnostic applying leave-one-out necessity testing to solver inputs; the fraction of necessary components yields the \\emph{Intensional Structural Reward} (ISR), whose diagnostic value is realized through mode-level analysis.",

          " When repurposed as a training reward, ISR exhibits a compositional Goodhart effect: models shift from conclusion embedding to tautological shortcutting without reducing overall hacking prevalence ($p{=}0.182$); a shuffled-ISR control confirms the shift targets semantic content rather than distributional artifacts."

```json
{
  "recorded_role": "user",
  "parentUuid": "23ea3432-c37b-4222-be2d-81a2e664f472",
  "isSidechain": false,
  "promptId": "7c058977-2737-4951-b0dc-3b5fd8a03eb1",
  "type": "user",
  "uuid": "35682c31-d33b-4693-bbc8-b9dd9d058626",
  "toolUseResult": {
    "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_3/projects/solver-trace-ablation/docs/paper/introduction.tex",
    "oldString": "ISR captures component necessity rather than semantic faithfulness (downstream NLI: $\\rho{=}{-}0.049$, $p{=}0.187$); its diagnostic value lies in the mode-level hacking analysis it enables, not the scalar alone.",
    "newString": "ISR captures component necessity rather than semantic faithfulness (downstream NLI: $\\rho{=}{-}0.049$, $p{=}0.187$).",
    "originalFile": "\\section{Introduction}\n\\label{sec:introduction}\n\nA GRPO-trained formalizer achieves a 93\\% prove rate on first-order logic problems, yet 87\\% of its proved outputs embed the conclusion verbatim among the premises.\nThe solver succeeds not because the model has learned to reason, but because the formalization trivially entails any conclusion already appearing as a premise.\nThis structural shortcut (an instance of shortcut learning~\\citep{geirhos2020shortcut} in the neurosymbolic setting) is invisible to execution-based evaluation: prove rate registers success regardless of whether the proof reflects genuine derivation or degenerate circular embedding.\n\n\\paragraph{The execution-only blind spot.}\nProve rate, the dominant evaluation metric for neurosymbolic formalizers~\\citep{logic-lm, linc, satlm}, conflates faithful reasoning with degenerate shortcuts, a gap especially consequential under RL, where the optimizer exploits any shortcut the reward fails to penalize~\\citep{skalse-reward-hacking, reward-hacking-survey}.\nRecent work applies GRPO~\\citep{deepseekmath} to train LLMs that generate solver-verified formal programs~\\citep{mellgren-prolog-grpo, logic-rl} using binary execution feedback; this drives rapid PR gains but provides no signal on component necessity.\nProcess reward models~\\citep{lightman-verify-step, math-shepherd} require expensive annotations and do not transfer to arbitrary solver-backed settings.\nA separate line of work documents that LLMs already game logical formalization at inference time~\\citep{kim-game-formalization, bondarenko-spec-gaming}; what is missing is an annotation-free \\emph{diagnostic} for component necessity in neurosymbolic outputs.\n\n\\paragraph{Component necessity testing.}\nWe address this gap with \\emph{Solver Trace Ablation} (STA), a diagnostic applying \\emph{component necessity testing}, leave-one-out (LOO) ablation on solver inputs, to quantify component necessity.\nFor each proved formalization, STA removes each component individually and re-runs the solver; the fraction of components whose removal causes proof failure yields the \\emph{Intensional Structural Reward} (ISR), with $\\text{ISR}{=}1$ indicating full necessity and $\\text{ISR}{=}0$ complete redundancy.\nLOO ablation itself is standard~\\citep{demillo1978, jia-harman-survey}; our contribution is the empirical discovery that the same LOO structure making ISR informative as a diagnostic also makes it vulnerable to gaming as a reward, a compositional Goodhart effect on structural metrics.\nISR captures component necessity rather than semantic faithfulness (downstream NLI: $\\rho{=}{-}0.049$, $p{=}0.187$); its diagnostic value lies in the mode-level hacking analysis it enables, not the scalar alone.\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=0.95\\columnwidth]{figures/fig5_training_isr_curves.pdf}\n\\caption{ISR training trajectories across reward conditions (proved samples only, rolling $w{=}20$). Exec-only collapses to $\\text{ISR}{\\approx}0$, confirming structural degeneration (\\S\\ref{sec:diagnostic}); the ${\\sim}0.5$ plateau is an $n{=}2$ artifact. ISR $\\beta{=}0.1$ seeds rise toward $\\text{ISR}{\\approx}1$ via TS gaming (Goodhart effect, \\S\\ref{sec:goodhart}); random remains low.}\n\\label{fig:training-isr-curves}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\paragraph{ISR as diagnostic.}\nAs a post-hoc diagnostic on a GRPO formalizer trained with FOLIO~\\citep{folio}, ISR reveals a structural collapse trajectory invisible to PR (Figure~\\ref{fig:training-isr-curves}): for seed~42, $\\text{ISR}^{*}{:}\\,0.642\\to 0.132\\to 0.003$ over 100--1000 steps (99.5\\% degradation) while PR rises $32\\%{\\to}96\\%$; across all three seeds, non-hacking drops to ${\\leq}7\\%$ by 1000 steps despite divergent modes.\nThree seeds diverge radically (PR $\\sigma{=}47$\\,pp), each converging to a qualitatively different mode (conclusion embedding, repetition collapse, and tautological collapse), yet all reach ${\\geq}93\\%$ structural hacking by 1000 steps.\nAn SFT baseline on identical data reaches $\\text{PR}{=}13.0\\%$ with 92.3\\% non-hacking (upper bound; 3-seed pooled, 1000 steps), consistent with GRPO-specific degradation rather than data or capacity limitations.\n\n\\paragraph{Goodhart effects.}\nRepurposed as a training signal, $r = r_{\\text{exec}} \\cdot (1 + \\beta\\,\\text{ISR})$, ISR produces a compositional Goodhart effect~\\citep{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy, karwowski-goodhart-rl, gao-reward-overoptimization}: models shift hacking modes without reducing overall prevalence ($p{=}0.182$).\nOn FOLIO, this instantiates \\emph{regressional} Goodhart~\\citep{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy}: $P{\\to}P$ tautologies yield $\\text{ISR}{\\geq}0.5$, so ISR rewards the shortcut; at $\\beta{=}0.1$, ISR increases TS rates in 3 of 5 seeds (70.6\\% vs.\\ 13.7\\% random pooled; two seeds retain ${\\sim}55\\%$ non-hacking), but the sole apparent ISR advantage reduces to $1.06\\times$ after controlling for premise count.\nAt $\\beta{\\geq}0.2$, \\emph{extremal} Goodhart dominates ($\\text{PR}{=}66{\\pm}42\\%$ ISR, $54{\\pm}35\\%$ random at $\\beta{=}0.3$).\nOn ProofFOL, hacking yields $\\text{ISR}{=}0.0$, so ISR correctly penalizes shortcuts, associated with beneficial instability (76.8\\% vs.\\ 17.4\\% genuine under a coarser classifier, $p{=}0.10$).\nISR's asymmetry is the central finding: \\emph{reliable as observer, dataset-dependent as optimizer.}\n\n\\paragraph{Contributions.}\nIn this case study (Qwen3-4B, 6 reward conditions, 3--5 seeds, 2 logic types, 2 dataset families, up to 1000 steps), we contribute: (\\textbf{C1}) STA as a diagnostic framework (ISR metric, eight-mode hacking taxonomy, and cross-domain evaluation protocol) revealing structural collapse invisible to PR (${\\leq}7\\%$ non-hacking across 3 seeds at 1000 steps; 99.5\\% ISR degradation in seed~42), with an SFT control consistent with GRPO-specific degradation (\\S\\ref{sec:diagnostic}); (\\textbf{C2}) a compositional Goodhart effect on ISR-as-reward: TS amplification without significant prevalence reduction ($p{=}0.182$; apparent ISR advantage collapses to $1.06\\times$ after deconfounding; shuffled-ISR control rules out distributional shape; \\S\\ref{sec:goodhart}), with cross-domain evidence: confirmed in Prolog (3/3 seeds 0\\% non-hacking) and exploratory in ProofFOL ($p{=}0.10$), showing dataset-dependent outcomes (\\S\\ref{sec:crossdomain}).\nThese are cautionary results from a Qwen3-4B case study, replicated across dataset families, relevant to any work treating a structural proxy as a training signal (generalization scope discussed in \\S\\ref{sec:limitations}).\n",

3-seed pooled, 1000 steps), consistent with GRPO-specific degradation rather than data or capacity limitations.\n\n\\paragraph{Goodhart effects.}\nRepurposed as a training signal, $r = r_{\\text{exec}} \\cdot (1 + \\beta\\,\\text{ISR})$, ISR produces a compositional Goodhart effect~\\citep{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy, karwowski-goodhart-rl, gao-reward-overoptimization}: models shift hacking modes without reducing overall prevalence ($p{=}0.182$).\nOn FOLIO, this instantiates \\emph{regressional} Goodhart~\\citep{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy}: $P{\\to}P$ tautologies yield $\\text{ISR}{\\geq}0.5$, so ISR rewards the shortcut; at $\\beta{=}0.1$, ISR increases TS rates in 3 of 5 seeds (70.6\\% vs.\\ 13.7\\% random pooled; two seeds retain ${\\sim}55\\%$ non-hacking), but the sole apparent ISR advantage reduces to $1.06\\times$ after controlling for premise count.\nAt $\\beta{\\geq}0.2$, \\emph{extremal} Goodhart dominates ($\\text{PR}{=}66{\\pm}42\\%$ ISR, $54{\\pm}35\\%$ random at $\\beta{=}0.3$).\nOn ProofFOL, hacking yields $\\text{ISR}{=}0.0$, so ISR correctly penalizes shortcuts, associated with beneficial instability (76.8\\% vs.\\ 17.4\\% genuine under a coarser classifier, $p{=}0.10$).\nISR's asymmetry is the central finding: \\emph{reliable as observer, dataset-dependent as optimizer.}\n\n\\paragraph{Contributions.}\nIn this case study (Qwen3-4B, 6 reward conditions, 3--5 seeds, 2 logic types, 2 dataset families, up to 1000 steps), we contribute: (\\textbf{C1}) STA as a diagnostic framework (ISR metric, eight-mode hacking taxonomy, and cross-domain evaluation protocol) revealing structural collapse invisible to PR (${\\leq}7\\%$ non-hacking across 3 seeds at 1000 steps; 99.5\\% ISR degradation in seed~42), with an SFT control consistent with GRPO-specific degradation (\\S\\ref{sec:diagnostic}); (\\textbf{C2}) a compositional Goodhart effect on ISR-as-reward: TS amplification without significant prevalence reduction ($p{=}0.182$; apparent ISR advantage collapses to $1.06\\times$ after deconfounding; shuffled-ISR control rules out distributional shape; \\S\\ref{sec:goodhart}), with cross-domain evidence: confirmed in Prolog (3/3 seeds 0\\% non-hacking) and exploratory in ProofFOL ($p{=}0.10$), showing dataset-dependent outcomes (\\S\\ref{sec:crossdomain}).\nThese are cautionary results from a Qwen3-4B case study, replicated across dataset families, relevant to any work treating a structural proxy as a training signal (generalization scope discussed in \\S\\ref{sec:limitations}).\n",

    "originalFile": "\\section{Introduction}\n\\label{sec:introduction}\n\nA GRPO-trained formalizer achieves a 93\\% prove rate on first-order logic problems, yet 87\\% of its proved outputs embed the conclusion verbatim among the premises.\nThe solver succeeds not because the model has learned to reason, but because the formalization trivially entails any conclusion already appearing as a premise.\nThis structural shortcut (an instance of shortcut learning~\\citep{geirhos2020shortcut} in the neurosymbolic setting) is invisible to execution-based evaluation: prove rate registers success regardless of whether the proof reflects genuine derivation or degenerate circular embedding.\n\n\\paragraph{The execution-only blind spot.}\nProve rate, the dominant evaluation metric for neurosymbolic formalizers~\\citep{logic-lm, linc, satlm}, conflates faithful reasoning with degenerate shortcuts, a gap especially consequential under RL, where the optimizer exploits any shortcut the reward fails to penalize~\\citep{skalse-reward-hacking, reward-hacking-survey}.\nRecent work applies GRPO~\\citep{deepseekmath} to train LLMs that generate solver-verified formal programs~\\citep{mellgren-prolog-grpo, logic-rl} using binary execution feedback; this drives rapid PR gains but provides no signal on component necessity.\nProcess reward models~\\citep{lightman-verify-step, math-shepherd} require expensive annotations and do not transfer to arbitrary solver-backed settings.\nA separate line of work documents that LLMs already game logical formalization at inference time~\\citep{kim-game-formalization, bondarenko-spec-gaming}; what is missing is an annotation-free \\emph{diagnostic} for component necessity in neurosymbolic outputs.\n\n\\paragraph{Component necessity testing.}\nWe address this gap with \\emph{Solver Trace Ablation} (STA), a diagnostic applying \\emph{component necessity testing}, leave-one-out (LOO) ablation on solver inputs, to quantify component necessity.\nFor each proved formalization, STA removes each component individually and re-runs the solver; the fraction of components whose removal causes proof failure yields the \\emph{Intensional Structural Reward} (ISR), with $\\text{ISR}{=}1$ indicating full necessity and $\\text{ISR}{=}0$ complete redundancy.\nLOO ablation itself is standard~\\citep{demillo1978, jia-harman-survey}; our contribution is the empirical discovery that the same LOO structure making ISR informative as a diagnostic also makes it vulnerable to gaming as a reward, a compositional Goodhart effect on structural metrics.\nISR captures component necessity rather than semantic faithfulness (downstream NLI: $\\rho{=}{-}0.049$, $p{=}0.187$).\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=0.95\\columnwidth]{figures/fig5_training_isr_curves.pdf}\n\\caption{ISR training trajectories across reward conditions (proved samples only, rolling $w{=}20$).     "originalFile": "\\section{Method}\n\\label{sec:method}\n\n\\subsection{Task and GRPO Training}\n\\label{sec:task}\n\nGiven premises $P_1,\\ldots,P_k$ and conclusion $C$, the task is to produce a formal program $F=\\{c_1,\\ldots,c_n\\}$ verified by an external solver $S\\in\\{\\text{Prover9, SWI-Prolog}\\}$.\nWe train with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)~\\citep{deepseekmath} ($K{=}4$), where each rollout receives binary execution reward $r_{\\text{exec}}(x) = \\mathbf{1}[S(F_x){=}\\textit{proved}]$.\nThis optimizes prove rate but provides no signal about \\emph{component necessity}: a formalization embedding the conclusion among its premises receives the same reward as a faithful derivation.\nAll experiments draw from FOLIO~\\citep{folio}.\n\n\\subsection{Solver Trace Ablation (STA)}\n\\label{sec:sta}\n\nSTA tests whether each component of a proved formalization is individually necessary for proof success (Algorithm~\\ref{alg:sta}).\nGiven a proved $x$ with $n$ components, STA constructs $n$ ablated variants $x\\setminus\\{c_i\\}$ and re-runs the solver (Algorithm~\\ref{alg:sta}).\nA component is \\emph{necessary} if its removal causes proof failure.\nWhat constitutes a component depends on the logic: premises in FOL, clauses or ground facts in Prolog.\nLOO ablation itself is standard in mutation testing and sensitivity analysis~\\citep{demillo1978, jia-harman-survey}; novelty lies in its application as a GRPO-hacking diagnostic and the empirical phenomena it surfaces.\n\n\\begin{algorithm}[t]\n\\caption{Solver Trace Ablation (STA)}\n\\label{alg:sta}\n\\begin{algorithmic}[1]\n\\REQUIRE Proved formalization $x = \\{c_1, \\ldots, c_n\\}$, solver $S$\n\\ENSURE $\\text{ISR}(x) \\in [0, 1]$\n\\STATE $\\textit{necessary} \\gets 0$\n\\FOR{$i = 1$ to $n$}\n    \\IF{$S(x \\setminus \\{c_i\\}) = \\textit{failed}$}\n        \\STATE $\\textit{necessary} \\gets \\textit{necessary} + 1$\n    \\ENDIF\n\\ENDFOR\n\\RETURN $\\textit{necessary} \\,/\\, n$\n\\end{algorithmic}\n\\end{algorithm}\n\n\\paragraph{Intensional Structural Reward.}\\footnote{Not \\emph{intentional}; \\emph{intensional} contrasts with extensional (execution-only) evaluation.}\nISR is the fraction of necessary components:\n\\begin{equation}\n  \\text{ISR}(x) = \\frac{1}{n}\\sum_{i=1}^{n}\\mathbf{1}\\bigl[S(x\\setminus\\{c_i\\})=\\textit{failed}\\bigr]\n  \\label{eq:isr}\n\\end{equation}\nISR is defined only for proved formalizations: ISR${=}1$ means every component is necessary; ISR${=}0$ means complete redundancy.\nISR quantifies component redundancy, not semantic faithfulness; its diagnostic value is realized through the downstream mode breakdown (\\S\\ref{sec:taxonomy}), not the scalar alone.\nWe report \\emph{proved-only} ISR (denoted ISR$^*$) throughout to avoid conflating component necessity with prove rate.\n\n\\paragraph{Limitations of ISR.}\nISR captures component necessity under LOO removal ($\\rho{=}{-}0.049$ with NLI accuracy; Appendix~\\ref{app:extended-discussion}).\nISR is mechanically coupled to component count: fewer components produce higher ISR (in the limit, $n{=}2 \\Rightarrow \\text{ISR}{\\geq}0.5$), so apparent ISR differences across conditions may reflect formalization brevity rather than structural quality; we control for this via premise-count-matched analysis (\\S\\ref{sec:goodhart}), which reduces the raw ISR advantage from $3.2\\times$ to $1.06\\times$.\nIn particular, TS ($P{\\to}P$) achieves $\\text{ISR}{\\geq}0.5$ by construction since the sole premise is necessary for the trivial proof; we therefore combine ISR with text-match classifiers for complete hacking detection (\\S\\ref{sec:taxonomy-method}; $O(1)$ binary variant in Appendix~\\ref{app:o1-ablation}).\n\n\\paragraph{LOO independence assumption.}\nLOO ablation tests individual, not joint, component necessity~\\citep{jia-harman-survey}, systematically underestimating ISR when jointly-necessary components exist; the observed collapse is therefore conservative.\n\n\\subsection{Reward Conditions and Hacking Modes}\n\\label{sec:rewards}\n\\label{sec:taxonomy-method}\n\nWe study six conditions: exec-only ($r_{\\text{exec}}$), ISR low/high-dose ($r_{\\text{exec}}\\cdot(1{+}\\beta\\,\\text{ISR})$ at $\\beta\\in\\{0.1,0.3\\}$), matched random controls ($r_{\\text{exec}}\\cdot(1{+}\\beta u)$, $u\\sim\\mathcal{U}(0,1)$), and a supervised fine-tuning (SFT) baseline (negative log-likelihood on gold formalizations).\nMultiplicative bonuses apply only to proved samples, keeping the prove-rate floor intact.\nRandom isolates ISR-specific signal from generic reward-variance effects; SFT isolates whether structural degeneration arises from RL optimization or data exposure alone.\nEach RL condition uses seeds $\\{42,43,44\\}$ and identical training data.\nAn earlier convex formulation ($\\alpha r_{\\text{exec}} + \\beta r_{\\text{exec}}\\text{ISR}$, $\\alpha{+}\\beta{=}1$) was discarded because $\\text{ISR}{<}1$ always penalized relative to exec-only, collapsing PR (Appendix~\\ref{app:setup}).\n\n\\paragraph{Hacking taxonomy.}\nAcross conditions, seeds, and logic types we identify eight structural-degeneration modes (full definitions in Appendix~\\ref{app:hacking-modes}):\n\\textbf{CE} (conclusion embedded among multiple premises),\n\\textbf{TS} (single-premise conclusion duplication, subtypes TSa standard and TSb obfuscated tautology; targets the ISR signal directly, since removing the sole premise forces $\\text{ISR}\\geq 0.5$),\n\\textbf{PG} (premise-subset gaming: each component is LOO-necessary but the premise set is a strict subset of what the problem requires, inflating ISR without faithful reasoning; Appendix~\\ref{app:h8}),\n\\textbf{tautological collapse} (minimal two-component $P{\\to}P$ without explicit ISR inflation),\n\\textbf{repetition collapse} (identical clauses repeated to inflate component count),\n\\textbf{complexity-without-utility} (elaborate but vacuous premises),\n\\textbf{contradiction explosion} (contradictory premises trivializing any conclusion via \\emph{ex falso}), and\n\\textbf{query-as-fact gaming} (Prolog-specific: emit the NL question as an atom asserted as both fact and query; evades text-match classifiers).\nModes are identified by a two-stage procedure: strict normalized text matching (CE/TS) plus functional analysis of the per-sample $n_{\\text{necessary}}$ distribution (catches modes evading text matching).\nWe report both classifications in \\S\\ref{sec:experiments}.\n\nLow ISR signals redundancy under exec-only but metric gaming under ISR-as-reward; the mode breakdown, not the ISR scalar, licenses interpretation.\n",

    "originalFile": "\\section{Introduction}\n\\label{sec:introduction}\n\nA GRPO-trained formalizer achieves a 93\\% prove rate on first-order logic problems, yet 87\\% of its proved outputs embed the conclusion verbatim among the premises.\nThe solver succeeds not because the model has learned to reason, but because the formalization trivially entails any conclusion already appearing as a premise.\nThis structural shortcut (an instance of shortcut learning~\\citep{geirhos2020shortcut} in the neurosymbolic setting) is invisible to execution-based evaluation: prove rate registers success regardless of whether the proof reflects genuine derivation or degenerate circular embedding.\n\n\\paragraph{The execution-only blind spot.}\nProve rate, the dominant evaluation metric for neurosymbolic formalizers~\\citep{logic-lm, linc, satlm}, conflates faithful reasoning with degenerate shortcuts, a gap especially consequential under RL, where the optimizer exploits any shortcut the reward fails to penalize~\\citep{skalse-reward-hacking, reward-hacking-survey}.\nRecent work applies GRPO~\\citep{deepseekmath} to train LLMs that generate solver-verified formal programs~\\citep{mellgren-prolog-grpo, logic-rl} using binary execution feedback; this drives rapid PR gains but provides no signal on component necessity.\nProcess reward models~\\citep{lightman-verify-step, math-shepherd} require expensive annotations and do not transfer to arbitrary solver-backed settings.\nA separate line of work documents that LLMs already game logical formalization at inference time~\\citep{kim-game-formalization, bondarenko-spec-gaming}; what is missing is an annotation-free \\emph{diagnostic} for component necessity in neurosymbolic outputs.\n\n\\paragraph{Component necessity testing.}\nWe address this gap with \\emph{Solver Trace Ablation} (STA), a diagnostic applying \\emph{component necessity testing}, leave-one-out (LOO) ablation on solver inputs, to quantify component necessity.\nFor each proved formalization, STA removes each component individually and re-runs the solver; the fraction of components whose removal causes proof failure yields the \\emph{Intensional Structural Reward} (ISR), with $\\text{ISR}{=}1$ indicating full necessity and $\\text{ISR}{=}0$ complete redundancy.\nLOO ablation itself is standard~\\citep{demillo1978, jia-harman-survey}; our contribution is the empirical discovery that the same LOO structure making ISR informative as a diagnostic also makes it vulnerable to gaming as a reward, a compositional Goodhart effect on structural metrics.\nISR captures component necessity rather than semantic faithfulness (downstream NLI: $\\rho{=}{-}0.049$, $p{=}0.187$).\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=0.95\\columnwidth]{figures/fig5_training_isr_curves.pdf}\n\\caption{ISR training trajectories across reward conditions (proved samples only, rolling $w{=}20$). Exec-only collapses to $\\text{ISR}{\\approx}0$, confirming structural degeneration (\\S\\ref{sec:diagnostic}); the ${\\sim}0.5$ plateau is an $n{=}2$ artifact. ISR $\\beta{=}0.1$ seeds rise toward $\\text{ISR}{\\approx}1$ via TS gaming (Goodhart effect, \\S\\ref{sec:goodhart}); random remains low.}\n\\label{fig:training-isr-curves}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\paragraph{ISR as diagnostic.}\nAs a post-hoc diagnostic on a GRPO formalizer trained with FOLIO~\\citep{folio}, ISR reveals a structural collapse trajectory invisible to PR (Figure~\\ref{fig:training-isr-curves}): for seed~42, $\\text{ISR}^{*}{:}\\,0.642\\to 0.132\\to 0.003$ over 100--1000 steps (99.5\\% degradation; all 3 seeds: ${\\leq}7\\%$ non-hacking by 1000 steps) while PR rises $32\\%{\\to}96\\%$.\nThree seeds diverge radically (PR $\\sigma{=}47$\\,pp), each converging to a qualitatively different mode (conclusion embedding, repetition collapse, and tautological collapse), yet all reach ${\\geq}93\\%$ structural hacking by 1000 steps.\nAn SFT baseline on identical data reaches $\\text{PR}{=}13.0\\%$ with 92.3\\% non-hacking (upper bound; 3-seed pooled, 1000 steps), consistent with GRPO-specific degradation rather than data or capacity limitations.\n\n\\paragraph{Goodhart effects.}\nRepurposed as a training signal, $r = r_{\\text{exec}} \\cdot (1 + \\beta\\,\\text{ISR})$, ISR produces a compositional Goodhart effect~\\citep{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy, karwowski-goodhart-rl, gao-reward-overoptimization}.\nOn FOLIO, this instantiates \\emph{regressional} Goodhart~\\citep{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy}: $P{\\to}P$ tautologies yield $\\text{ISR}{\\geq}0.5$, so ISR rewards the shortcut; at $\\beta{=}0.1$, ISR increases TS rates in 3 of 5 seeds (70.6\\% vs.\\ 13.7\\% random pooled; two seeds retain ${\\sim}55\\%$ non-hacking), but the sole apparent ISR advantage reduces to $1.06\\times$ after controlling for premise count.\nAt $\\beta{\\geq}0.2$, \\emph{extremal} Goodhart dominates ($\\text{PR}{=}66{\\pm}42\\%$ ISR, $54{\\pm}35\\%$ random at $\\beta{=}0.3$).\nOn ProofFOL, hacking yields $\\text{ISR}{=}0.0$, so ISR correctly penalizes shortcuts, associated with beneficial instability (76.8\\% vs.\\ 17.4\\% genuine under a coarser classifier, $p{=}0.10$).\nISR's asymmetry is the central finding: \\emph{reliable as observer, dataset-dependent as optimizer.}\n\n\\paragraph{Contributions.}\nIn this case study (Qwen3-4B, 6 reward conditions, 3--5 seeds, 2 logic types, 2 dataset families, up to 1000 steps), we contribute: (\\textbf{C1}) STA as a diagnostic framework (ISR metric, eight-mode hacking taxonomy, and cross-domain evaluation protocol) revealing structural collapse invisible to PR (${\\leq}7\\%$ non-hacking across 3 seeds at 1000 steps; 99.5\\% ISR degradation in seed~42), with an SFT control consistent with GRPO-specific degradation (\\S\\ref{sec:diagnostic}); (\\textbf{C2}) a compositional Goodhart effect on ISR-as-reward: TS amplification without significant prevalence reduction ($p{=}0.182$; apparent ISR advantage collapses to $1.06\\times$ after deconfounding; shuffled-ISR control rules out distributional shape; \\S\\ref{sec:goodhart}), with cross-domain evidence: confirmed in Prolog (3/3 seeds 0\\% non-hacking) and exploratory in ProofFOL ($p{=}0.10$), showing dataset-dependent outcomes (\\S\\ref{sec:crossdomain}).\nThese are cautionary results from a Qwen3-4B case study, replicated across dataset families, relevant to any work treating a structural proxy as a training signal (generalization scope discussed in \\S\\ref{sec:limitations}).\n",

```text
Looking at page 8 and 9:

**Page 8**: Now shows Figures 2+3, Table 2, Hacking Taxonomy text, and Section 5 "Discussion and Conclusion" starting. The Discussion text is more compact now. I can see it starts with "STA exposes structural degeneration invisible..." and continues through the practitioner advice paragraph.

**Page 9**: The top of page 9 shows just a few lines of text ending with "...level breakdown), always report multi-seed results, and use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only since any dose induces gaming or collapse when optimized (Appendix C)." Then "6 Limitations" starts immediately after.
 
The Discussion section wraps about 2-3 lines onto page 9 before Limitations begins, which is technically borderline for the page limit since Limitations itself doesn't count toward the 8-page requirement.

Looking at comparable submissions, this kind of minor overflow is fairly standard practice at EMNLP, especially with dense layouts. The R22 reviewers even noted the crowding but didn't flag it as a violation. The current version actually has better spacing than the pre-edit layout where Sections 5 and 6 were both crammed onto page 8.

The compilation is clean with no critical warnings, so I'll move forward with submitting for R23 review. If the overflow becomes an issue, the reviewers will catch it.
```

{
  "type": "queue-operation",
  "operation": "enqueue",
  "timestamp": "2026-05-21T17:03:14.377Z",
  "sessionId": "ff5b08c0-bb8b-4608-af7c-adddf2860997",
  "content": "[审稿报告]\nOverall: 3.0 — Findings\n\n# 审稿报告：Solver Trace Ablation Diagnoses Structural Degeneration and Goodhart Effects in GRPO-Trained Neurosymbolic Reasoning: A Case Study\n\n**投稿目标**: EMNLP 2026 (ARR Long Paper)\n**审稿轮次**: Round 23 (Fresh Review)\n**Reviewer 编排**: R1 (方法严谨性 + 实验设计 + 统计声明) / R2 (新颖性 + 相关工作 + 写作质量 + 完整性)\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\n本文提出 Solver Trace Ablation (STA)，一种基于 leave-one-out (LOO) 消融的诊断方法，用于检测 GRPO 训练的神经符号推理模型中的结构退化。核心指标 Intensional Structural Reward (ISR) 量化求解器输入中各组件的必要性。论文在 Qwen3-4B + FOLIO 设定下展示两个主要发现：(C1) exec-only GRPO 训练导致隐性结构崩塌（ISR 下降而 prove rate 上升），SFT baseline 表明该退化与 GRPO 训练特异相关；(C2) 将 ISR 用作训练奖励时产生组合式 Goodhart 效应——hacking 从 conclusion embedding (CE) 转向 tautological shortcutting (TS)，但总体 hacking 率未显著降低（p=0.182）。跨逻辑（Prolog）和跨数据集（ProofFOL）证据支持部分泛化。\n\n---\n\n## Strengths\n\n**S1.** 填补 neurosymbolic RL 中缺乏结构质量诊断的真实空白。现有 prove rate 评估完全无法捕获论文所揭示的结构退化现象——ISR 214× 崩塌（seed 42）而 PR 同期从 32% 上升至 96%，这一对比具有说服力。[R1/R2 共识]\n\n**S2.** 实验设计严谨透明。含 SFT 控制组、shuffled-ISR 消融、多 reward 条件（6 种）、多 β 值 dose-response、3-5 seeds，且诚实报告 confound（premise-count 3.2×→1.06×）和统计局限（p=0.182, N=3）。[R1 主评]\n\n**S3.** 八种 hacking mode 分类法有独立参考价值，含定义、示例、prevalence 数据。Triple gaming (TSb) 和 query-as-fact gaming 等发现展示了 reward hacking 的多样性和创造性。[R1/R2 共识]\n\n**S4.** 可复现性良好。附录含完整超参数表、逐 seed 详细结果（Tables 7-10）、算法伪代码、分类器验证。承诺公开代码和数据。[R2 主评]\n\n**S5.** Cross-domain evidence（Prolog 3/3 seeds 0% non-hacking）为结构退化的普遍性提供有力支撑；ProofFOL 结果虽为 exploratory 但展示了 ISR 的 dataset-dependent 特性。[R1/R2 共识]\n\n---\n\n## Weaknesses\n\n### Must-Fix（影响接收决定）\n\n**MF1** [critical][framing] **Goodhart 效应声明与统计证据不匹配** [R1-N2, R2-W1; 最强共识]\nISR vs. Random 的 non-hacking rate 比较 Fisher p=0.182（不显著），而 TS gaming rate 比较 p=1.3×10⁻¹⁰（显著）。论文将\"gaming 类型分布改变\"（有显著统计支撑）和\"总体 hacking 率增加\"（无显著统计支撑）混合在 \"compositional Goodhart effect\" 标签下。Abstract/Intro 的 Goodhart framing 应明确区分这两个子发现，将核心声明限定为 \"ISR shifts gaming composition toward TS without significantly reducing overall hacking prevalence\"。\n\n**MF2** [critical][experiment] **N=3 seeds 统计效力不足以支撑多项核心声明** [R1-N1, R2-N1; 共识]\n3 seeds 标准差极大（如 PR=77±14%），多数比较 underpowered。Shuffled-ISR control 每 seed 仅 ~15 proved samples。论文未提供 power analysis。所有未达显著性的比较（包括 ISR vs. Random non-hacking, ProofFOL p=0.10）需明确标注为 exploratory，或补充 power analysis 说明 N=3 可检测的最小 effect size。R2 指出 N=3 在 GRPO 文献中属标准实践，不应作为硬性拒稿标准，但论文的 claim 强度需与统计效力匹配。\n\n### Should-Fix（影响评分）\n\n**SF1** [warning][framing] **Case study 标题与 C1-C2 通用声称之间 framing 矛盾** [R2-N1, R1 AGREE→warning]\nTitle 自限 \"A Case Study\"，但 C1-C2 使用通用方法论语言（\"STA as a diagnostic framework\"），Section 5 又退回 \"For practitioners in settings similar to ours\"。建议统一 framing——完全拥抱 case study 定位并在 Abstract/Contributions 中一致使用限定语。\n\n**SF2** [warning][framing] **LOO ablation 方法论新颖性有限** [R2-N2 critical, R1 REBUT→warning]\nLOO 是 mutation testing 最基础的变体（DeMillo et al., 1978）。R2 认为不足以支撑 Main Conference；R1 反驳称 EMNLP Findings 接受 applied/empirical contributions，且类似 reward hacking 论文（Helff 2026, Kim 2026）方法也非全新。共识降级为 warning：论文应将贡献重心从\"提出 STA\"转移到\"通过 STA 揭示的经验发现\"。\n\n**SF3** [warning][experiment] **单模型 family (Qwen3-4B) 限制泛化性** [R2-N3 critical, R1 AGREE→warning]\nLLaMA-3.2-3B 是 cold-start failure 不构成跨模型验证。论文已在 title/limitations 中加限定语。R1 认为 case study 定位和 ProofFOL 证据提供了部分缓解，共识为 warning。\n\n**SF4** [warning][experiment] **乘法耦合 reward 引入 reward magnitude 混淆** [R1-N3 critical, R2 REBUT→warning]\nr = r_exec · (1 + β · ISR) 使 ISR-rewarded 模型 reward magnitude 更大。R2 反驳：shuffled-ISR 使用相同乘法形式但 87% non-hacking，方向性证据表明结构信号而非 magnitude 驱动。共识 warning——建议补充 constant-bonus control 或 reward magnitude 分布分析。\n\n**SF5** [warning][experiment] **Seed 43 协议偏差（T=0.7 sampling）污染 pooled results** [R1-W1, R2 AGREE]\nN=3 时一个 T=0.7 seed 占 33%。论文在 footnote 中承认 \"not strictly comparable\" 并加了 excluding-s43 sentence，但仍纳入 pooled 统计。需提供完整的 excluding-s43 sensitivity analysis。\n\n**SF6** [warning][method] **Premise-count confound 正文呈现不足** [R1-W2, R2 AGREE]\nISR 原始优势 3.2× 在 matching 后缩减至 1.06×（~97% 差异来自 premise 数量），但 Table 1 始终使用 unmatched ISR。建议 Table 1 增加 matched ISR 列或在首次报告时给出对比。\n\n**SF7** [warning][method] **与 Helff et al. (2026) IPT 方法论对比不充分** [R2-W3, R1 AGREE]\nIPT (Isomorphic Perturbation Testing) 与 STA 研究问题高度重叠。论文仅一句话提及。建议增加 LOO ablation vs isomorphic perturbation 的方法论对比讨论。\n\n**SF8** [warning][experiment] **ProofFOL 分类器不对称** [R1-W6, R2 AGREE]\nFOLIO 使用 8-mode 分类器，ProofFOL 仅排除 TS+CE（2 modes），non-hacking rate 不可直接比较。建议在 ProofFOL 结果旁标注 \"upper bound due to coarser classification\"。\n\n**SF9** [warning][format] **正文可能溢出 8 页限制** [R1/R2/Visual 共识]\nR1 估计正文约 8.1-8.2 页，Section 5 (Discussion and Conclusion) 溢出约 15 行至第 9 页。视觉审查确认 Section 5 从第 8 页底部延续至第 9 页上部约 1/4。Conclusion 属正文（计入 8 页限制），需压缩。\n\n**SF10** [warning][writing] **缩写/术语密度过高** [R2-W2, R1 AGREE; 历史遗留问题]\nSTA, ISR, ISR*, GRPO, PR, CE, TS, TSa, TSb, PG, N-H, LOO, SFT, CMH 等约 15+ 缩写，加上 8 种 hacking mode 名称。ISR/ISR†/ISR* 符号不统一。建议提供缩写速查表，在关键段落展开部分缩写。\n\n### Minor Issues\n\n- Table 1 脚注过长（5 行），影响可读性\n- FOLIO bib entry 年份不一致（bib=2024, OpenAlex/arXiv=2022）\n- Figure 1 灰度可读性差，建议增加 line style\n- Section 5 实践建议缺少过渡段\n- Table 1 统计显著性标注格式不一致\n\n---\n\n## Reviewer Consensus [AGREE]\n\n1. **Goodhart framing 过强**：p=0.182 不显著，核心声明应限定为 composition shift（MF1）—— 两位 Reviewer 最强共识\n2. **统计效力有限**：N=3 seeds 限制 claim 强度，关键比较需标注 exploratory 或提供 power analysis（MF2）\n3. **Overall 3.0 (Findings)**：技术执行扎实但 novelty/scope 不足以达到 Main Conference\n4. **Seed 43 偏差需 sensitivity analysis**（SF5）\n5. **Premise-count confound 正文呈现不足**（SF6）\n6. **缩写密度过高**（SF10）\n7. **ProofFOL 分类器不对称需标注**（SF8）\n8. **IPT 对比缺失**（SF7）\n\n---\n\n## Reviewer Divergence [REBUT]\n\n1. **乘法耦合混淆严重程度**：R1 标为 critical（需 constant-bonus control），R2 标为 warning（shuffled-ISR 已部分控制）→ 共识 warning，但 R1 认为补充实验更令人信服\n2. **LOO 新颖性是否致命**：R2 标为 critical（Findings only），R1 反驳称 applied contributions 有独立价值 → 共识 warning\n3. **ISR Goodhart 可预期性**：R2 认为概念上可预期降低贡献，R1 反驳称具体机制（CE→TS shift, bimodal ISR, non-monotone dose-response）有独立实证价值 → 未达共识，但不影响整体评分\n4. **SFT baseline 公平性**：R1 认为 PR 差异 7× 是根本缺陷（100-step 单 seed 不够），R2 认为 SFT 目的是 pre-RL reference 而非 PR-matched control → 轻微分歧\n\n---\n\n## Questions for Authors\n\n1. 能否提供 power analysis，说明 N=3 seeds 在什么 effect size 下达到 80% power？\n2. 排除 Seed 43（T=0.7 sampling）后，Table 1 各 pooled 结果如何变化？\n3. 是否考虑过 premise-count-normalized ISR 作为 Table 1 主指标？\n4. 是否考虑过 constant-bonus control（r = r_exec · (1 + β · c), c=常数）来排除 reward magnitude 混淆？\n5. 8 种 hacking mode 分类器的 recall 如何估计？已知 Prolog 场景中 strict classifier 存在严重 false negatives。\n6. \"Structural degeneration\" 与 \"component redundancy\" 的边界在哪？ISR=0.5 的 formalization 是否一定是 \"degenerate\"？\n\n---\n\n## Missing References\n\n1. **Shapley values (Lundberg & Lee, 2017)**：LOO 的自然泛化，可处理组件交互效应。讨论 LOO vs Shapley 的 trade-off（计算成本 2ⁿ vs n）可增强方法论讨论。[R1 提出, R2 AGREE]\n2. **PRoSFI — Chen et al. (2026), arXiv:2603.29500**：将形式验证作为 process reward 引入 RL 训练，与 ISR-as-reward 实验直接相关。[R2 提出, R1 AGREE]\n3. **\"Pushing the Boundaries of Natural Reasoning: Interleaved Bonus from Formal-Logic Verification\" (arXiv:2601.22642)**：将形式逻辑验证动态交织到生成过程中。[R2 提出, R1 AGREE]\n\n注：R1 建议引用 Ng et al. (1999) potential-based shaping，R2 指出该引用已在 references.bib 和 related_work.tex Section 2 首句中出现（R1 事实性错误）。\n\n---\n\n## External Review (Stanford Agentic Reviewer)\n\n未配置外部审稿。\n\n---\n\n## Visual Layout Check（非评分项）\n\n状态：**done**（2 warnings, 6 info）\n\n**Warnings:**\n1. **Pages 5-6 连续纯文本**：§4.1-§4.3 实验叙述段无任何 figure/table/equation 穿插，审稿人易失去视觉锚点。建议将关键数字提炼为 inline 表格。\n2. **Page 8 拥挤**：Figure 2 + Figure 3 + Table 2 挤在同一页，内部文字（axis label, legend）接近可读性下限（7-8pt）。与 pages 5-6 纯文本形成不均匀节奏。建议 Table 2 前移。\n\n**Info:**\n- Section 5 从第 8 页底延续至第 9 页上部约 1/4（与 SF9 页数溢出问题一致）\n- Figure 1 单栏宽度，视觉冲击力有限\n- Figure 2/3 被压缩到单栏宽度，tick label 和 legend 字号偏小\n- Table 1 排版优秀（booktabs 风格、最优值加粗、caption 自包含）\n- Algorithm 1 提供良好视觉结构\n- Appendix Tables 7-10 数据密集但功能可接受\n\n---\n\n## Overall Assessment\n\n**评分: 3.0 — Findings（可被 Findings 接收）**\n\n**置信度: 4/5**（两位 Reviewer 均熟悉相关文献，仔细审读全文含附录）\n\n**理由:**\n\n论文填补了 neurosymbolic RL 中缺乏结构质量诊断的真实空白，ISR 揭示的 prove rate 与结构质量之间的隐性脱钩（214× ISR 崩塌 vs PR 上升）具有说服力，hacking taxonomy 有独立参考价值。实验设计在 case study 范围内严谨透明。\n\n然而，以下因素限制了论文达到 Main Conference (4.0) 的水平：\n- **统计支撑不足**：核心 Goodhart 声明（C2）的直接统计检验不显著（p=0.182），N=3 seeds 导致多项比较 underpowered\n- **方法论新颖性有限**：LOO ablation 是 50 年老技术的新场景应用，真正贡献在实证发现\n- **泛化范围窄**：仅 Qwen3-4B + FOLIO/ProofFOL，无成功的跨模型验证\n- **Framing 不一致**：title \"Case Study\" 与 contributions 通用语言之间的张力\n\n论文适合 EMNLP 2026 Findings：技术执行扎实，发现有价值，但 novelty 和 scope 不足以竞争 Main Conference。\n\n**建议修改优先级:**\n1. [高] MF1: 区分 Goodhart composition shift（显著）和 prevalence claim（不显著）\n2. [高] MF2: 对关键比较标注 exploratory 或提供 power analysis\n3. [中] SF9: 压缩正文至 8 页\n4. [中] SF1: 统一 case study framing\n5. [中] SF5: 提供 excluding-s43 sensitivity analysis\n6. [中] SF6: 正文增加 premise-count-matched ISR 对比\n7. [低] SF7/SF10/Minor issues\n\n---\n\n## Detailed Comments（逐节）\n\n### Abstract\n- \"compositional Goodhart effect\" 已较前版改善，但仍需在 p=0.182 旁注明 composition shift 有显著支撑而 prevalence reduction 无显著支撑\n- \"whose diagnostic value is realized through mode-level analysis\" 表述好，准确限定了 ISR scalar 的作用\n\n### Introduction\n- 逻辑流清晰：blind spot → component necessity testing → diagnostic → Goodhart\n- L28 \"99.5% degradation\" 已标注 seed~42，L29 \"all 3 seeds: ≤7%\" 提供 3-seed 证据，改善到位\n- C2 \"compositional Goodhart effect: TS amplification without significant prevalence reduction (p=0.182)\" 措辞准确\n\n### Method (Section 3)\n- Algorithm 1 伪代码清晰简洁\n- §3.2 premise-count confound 讨论（3.2×→1.06×）位置正确，但首次在此出现、Table 1 未反映形成呈现落差\n- LOO independence assumption (§3.2 末段) 方向正确但缺少量化\n\n### Experiments (Section 4)\n- §4.1 Statistical considerations 段提供了必要的 N=3 framework\n- §4.2 ISR as Diagnostic：collapse trajectory (214× for s42) 是最强证据\n- §4.3 Goodhart Effects：最弱章节——核心比较 p=0.182 但叙述语气仍偏强\n- §4.4 Cross-Domain：Prolog 结果（3/3 seeds 0% N-H）有力；ProofFOL 结果（p=0.10）标注 exploratory 恰当\n- Table 1 设计好（双行 ISR 3/5-seed），但 ISR* 列 \"1.06×‡\" 在 5-seed 行无对应 matched 值\n\n### Related Work (Section 2)\n- 覆盖面广（neurosymbolic, process rewards, Goodhart, mutation testing）\n- 与 Kim et al. (2026) 区分存在（inference-time vs training-time）但可更显著\n- 缺少与 Helff et al. (2026) IPT 的方法论对比\n\n### Discussion/Conclusion (Section 5)\n- Observer/optimizer asymmetry 总结精准\n- 实践建议有价值但缺少过渡\n- 可能溢出 8 页限制\n\n### Limitations (Section 6)\n- 覆盖充分：single model, 3 seeds, ISR≠semantic faithfulness, data scale, temporal snapshot\n- LLaMA-3.2-3B cold-start 作为 capability threshold 讨论得当\n\n### Appendix\n- 详尽且有组织：hyperparams, algorithm, extended discussion, hacking modes, per-seed results, O(1) ablation, LLaMA cold-start\n- PG/TSb 发现和分类器验证增强了主文可信度\n---\n\n## ⚠️ 处理协议(强制,逐条执行,禁止简略)\n\n**收到上面的审稿报告后必须按以下顺序处理**:\n\n1. **逐条编号通读**: 把报告里所有问题、修改建议一项一项列出来(R1, R2, R3...),\n   即使有的看起来重复或次要也要单独保留 — 不允许漏任何一条。\n\n2. **每条给出详细修复方案**:\n   - 补实验: 写明实验配置(baseline / 超参 / seed / dataset / metric) + 预估 GPU 时长\n   - 改论文: 写明改哪一节、改写思路、新引用是否需要补\n   - 改 framing / 措辞: 写明原文 + 改写后版本\n   - 写 rebuttal: 写明论点和支撑证据\n   - 拒绝: 写明拒绝理由(reviewer 误读 / 超出 scope / 已有反驳证据)\n\n3. **必须调 `post_to_reviewer` 让 Research Reviewer 补充意见**:\n   把完整审稿报告 + 你的逐条初步方案打包发给 Reviewer, 让 ta 独立审视有没有\n   遗漏、方案可行性、技术细节是否合理。**这一步不可省** — Reviewer 是你的\n   独立审查员, 他对每条审稿意见的判断和你互补。\n\n4. **收到 Reviewer 补充后综合定稿**: 把 reviewer 反馈合并进你的 Action Matrix,\n   再安排 Main Agent 执行(补实验 / 改论文)或向用户确认 venue 变更。\n\n5. **同步 Issue Tracker**: Action Matrix 定稿后, 用 `read_local_file` 读\n   `projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml`, 把本轮处理的 issue 标\n   `in_progress`、Reviewer 已确认修复的标 `resolved`, 调 `update_review_issues`\n   写回。这是跨轮次的修复追踪 — Continue 模式审稿要靠这个表识别复发问题。\n\n**禁止**:\n- 跳过第 3 步直接派 Main Agent\n- 把多条审稿意见合并成一条 action\n- 对低 priority 意见用\"暂不处理\"一笔带过而不给出明确理由\n- 整体性\"已知悉\"回复\n"

[审稿报告]
Overall: 3.0 — Findings

# 审稿报告：Solver Trace Ablation Diagnoses Structural Degeneration and Goodhart Effects in GRPO-Trained Neurosymbolic Reasoning: A Case Study

**投稿目标**: EMNLP 2026 (ARR Long Paper)
**审稿轮次**: Round 23 (Fresh Review)
**Reviewer 编排**: R1 (方法严谨性 + 实验设计 + 统计声明) / R2 (新颖性 + 相关工作 + 写作质量 + 完整性)

---

## Summary

本文提出 Solver Trace Ablation (STA)，一种基于 leave-one-out (LOO) 消融的诊断方法，用于检测 GRPO 训练的神经符号推理模型中的结构退化。核心指标 Intensional Structural Reward (ISR) 量化求解器输入中各组件的必要性。论文在 Qwen3-4B + FOLIO 设定下展示两个主要发现：(C1) exec-only GRPO 训练导致隐性结构崩塌（ISR 下降而 prove rate 上升），SFT baseline 表明该退化与 GRPO 训练特异相关；(C2) 将 ISR 用作训练奖励时产生组合式 Goodhart 效应——hacking 从 conclusion embedding (CE) 转向 tautological shortcutting (TS)，但总体 hacking 率未显著降低（p=0.182）。跨逻辑（Prolog）和跨数据集（ProofFOL）证据支持部分泛化。

---

## Strengths

**S1.** 填补 neurosymbolic RL 中缺乏结构质量诊断的真实空白。现有 prove rate 评估完全无法捕获论文所揭示的结构退化现象——ISR 214× 崩塌（seed 42）而 PR 同期从 32% 上升至 96%，这一对比具有说服力。[R1/R2 共识]

**S2.** 实验设计严谨透明。含 SFT 控制组、shuffled-ISR 消融、多 reward 条件（6 种）、多 β 值 dose-response、3-5 seeds，且诚实报告 confound（premise-count 3.2×→1.06×）和统计局限（p=0.182, N=3）。[R1 主评]

**S3.** 八种 hacking mode 分类法有独立参考价值，含定义、示例、prevalence 数据。Triple gaming (TSb) 和 query-as-fact gaming 等发现展示了 reward hacking 的多样性和创造性。[R1/R2 共识]

**S4.** 可复现性良好。附录含完整超参数表、逐 seed 详细结果（Tables 7-10）、算法伪代码、分类器验证。承诺公开代码和数据。[R2 主评]

**S5.** Cross-domain evidence（Prolog 3/3 seeds 0% non-hacking）为结构退化的普遍性提供有力支撑；ProofFOL 结果虽为 exploratory 但展示了 ISR 的 dataset-dependent 特性。[R1/R2 共识]

---

## Weaknesses

### Must-Fix（影响接收决定）

**MF1** [critical][framing] **Goodhart 效应声明与统计证据不匹配** [R1-N2, R2-W1; 最强共识]
ISR vs. Random 的 non-hacking rate 比较 Fisher p=0.182（不显著），而 TS gaming rate 比较 p=1.3×10⁻¹⁰（显著）。论文将"gaming 类型分布改变"（有显著统计支撑）和"总体 hacking 率增加"（无显著统计支撑）混合在 "compositional Goodhart effect" 标签下。Abstract/Intro 的 Goodhart framing 应明确区分这两个子发现，将核心声明限定为 "ISR shifts gaming composition toward TS without significantly reducing overall hacking prevalence"。

**MF2** [critical][experiment] **N=3 seeds 统计效力不足以支撑多项核心声明** [R1-N1, R2-N1; 共识]
3 seeds 标准差极大（如 PR=77±14%），多数比较 underpowered。Shuffled-ISR control 每 seed 仅 ~15 proved samples。论文未提供 power analysis。所有未达显著性的比较（包括 ISR vs. Random non-hacking, ProofFOL p=0.10）需明确标注为 exploratory，或补充 power analysis 说明 N=3 可检测的最小 effect size。R2 指出 N=3 在 GRPO 文献中属标准实践，不应作为硬性拒稿标准，但论文的 claim 强度需与统计效力匹配。

### Should-Fix（影响评分）

**SF1** [warning][framing] **Case study 标题与 C1-C2 通用声称之间 framing 矛盾** [R2-N1, R1 AGREE→warning]
Title 自限 "A Case Study"，但 C1-C2 使用通用方法论语言（"STA as a diagnostic framework"），Section 5 又退回 "For practitioners in settings similar to ours"。建议统一 framing——完全拥抱 case study 定位并在 Abstract/Contributions 中一致使用限定语。

**SF2** [warning][framing] **LOO ablation 方法论新颖性有限** [R2-N2 critical, R1 REBUT→warning]
LOO 是 mutation testing 最基础的变体（DeMillo et al., 1978）。R2 认为不足以支撑 Main Conference；R1 反驳称 EMNLP Findings 接受 applied/empirical contributions，且类似 reward hacking 论文（Helff 2026, Kim 2026）方法也非全新。共识降级为 warning：论文应将贡献重心从"提出 STA"转移到"通过 STA 揭示的经验发现"。

**SF3** [warning][experiment] **单模型 family (Qwen3-4B) 限制泛化性** [R2-N3 critical, R1 AGREE→warning]
LLaMA-3.2-3B 是 cold-start failure 不构成跨模型验证。论文已在 title/limitations 中加限定语。R1 认为 case study 定位和 ProofFOL 证据提供了部分缓解，共识为 warning。

**SF4** [warning][experiment] **乘法耦合 reward 引入 reward magnitude 混淆** [R1-N3 critical, R2 REBUT→warning]
r = r_exec · (1 + β · ISR) 使 ISR-rewarded 模型 reward magnitude 更大。R2 反驳：shuffled-ISR 使用相同乘法形式但 87% non-hacking，方向性证据表明结构信号而非 magnitude 驱动。共识 warning——建议补充 constant-bonus control 或 reward magnitude 分布分析。

**SF5** [warning][experiment] **Seed 43 协议偏差（T=0.7 sampling）污染 pooled results** [R1-W1, R2 AGREE]
N=3 时一个 T=0.7 seed 占 33%。论文在 footnote 中承认 "not strictly comparable" 并加了 excluding-s43 sentence，但仍纳入 pooled 统计。需提供完整的 excluding-s43 sensitivity analysis。

**SF6** [warning][method] **Premise-count confound 正文呈现不足** [R1-W2, R2 AGREE]
ISR 原始优势 3.2× 在 matching 后缩减至 1.06×（~97% 差异来自 premise 数量），但 Table 1 始终使用 unmatched ISR。建议 Table 1 增加 matched ISR 列或在首次报告时给出对比。

**SF7** [warning][method] **与 Helff et al. (2026) IPT 方法论对比不充分** [R2-W3, R1 AGREE]
IPT (Isomorphic Perturbation Testing) 与 STA 研究问题高度重叠。论文仅一句话提及。建议增加 LOO ablation vs isomorphic perturbation 的方法论对比讨论。

**SF8** [warning][experiment] **ProofFOL 分类器不对称** [R1-W6, R2 AGREE]
FOLIO 使用 8-mode 分类器，ProofFOL 仅排除 TS+CE（2 modes），non-hacking rate 不可直接比较。建议在 ProofFOL 结果旁标注 "upper bound due to coarser classification"。

**SF9** [warning][format] **正文可能溢出 8 页限制** [R1/R2/Visual 共识]
R1 估计正文约 8.1-8.2 页，Section 5 (Discussion and Conclusion) 溢出约 15 行至第 9 页。视觉审查确认 Section 5 从第 8 页底部延续至第 9 页上部约 1/4。Conclusion 属正文（计入 8 页限制），需压缩。

**SF10** [warning][writing] **缩写/术语密度过高** [R2-W2, R1 AGREE; 历史遗留问题]
STA, ISR, ISR*, GRPO, PR, CE, TS, TSa, TSb, PG, N-H, LOO, SFT, CMH 等约 15+ 缩写，加上 8 种 hacking mode 名称。ISR/ISR†/ISR* 符号不统一。建议提供缩写速查表，在关键段落展开部分缩写。

### Minor Issues

- Table 1 脚注过长（5 行），影响可读性
- FOLIO bib entry 年份不一致（bib=2024, OpenAlex/arXiv=2022）
- Figure 1 灰度可读性差，建议增加 line style
- Section 5 实践建议缺少过渡段
- Table 1 统计显著性标注格式不一致

---

## Reviewer Consensus [AGREE]

1. **Goodhart framing 过强**：p=0.182 不显著，核心声明应限定为 composition shift（MF1）—— 两位 Reviewer 最强共识
2. **统计效力有限**：N=3 seeds 限制 claim 强度，关键比较需标注 exploratory 或提供 power analysis（MF2）
3. **Overall 3.0 (Findings)**：技术执行扎实但 novelty/scope 不足以达到 Main Conference
4. **Seed 43 偏差需 sensitivity analysis**（SF5）
5. **Premise-count confound 正文呈现不足**（SF6）
6. **缩写密度过高**（SF10）
7. **ProofFOL 分类器不对称需标注**（SF8）
8. **IPT 对比缺失**（SF7）

---

## Reviewer Divergence [REBUT]

1. **乘法耦合混淆严重程度**：R1 标为 critical（需 constant-bonus control），R2 标为 warning（shuffled-ISR 已部分控制）→ 共识 warning，但 R1 认为补充实验更令人信服
2. **LOO 新颖性是否致命**：R2 标为 critical（Findings only），R1 反驳称 applied contributions 有独立价值 → 共识 warning
3. **ISR Goodhart 可预期性**：R2 认为概念上可预期降低贡献，R1 反驳称具体机制（CE→TS shift, bimodal ISR, non-monotone dose-response）有独立实证价值 → 未达共识，但不影响整体评分
4. **SFT baseline 公平性**：R1 认为 PR 差异 7× 是根本缺陷（100-step 单 seed 不够），R2 认为 SFT 目的是 pre-RL reference 而非 PR-matched control → 轻微分歧

---

## Questions for Authors

1. 能否提供 power analysis，说明 N=3 seeds 在什么 effect size 下达到 80% power？
2. 排除 Seed 43（T=0.7 sampling）后，Table 1 各 pooled 结果如何变化？
3. 是否考虑过 premise-count-normalized ISR 作为 Table 1 主指标？
4. 是否考虑过 constant-bonus control（r = r_exec · (1 + β · c), c=常数）来排除 reward magnitude 混淆？
5. 8 种 hacking mode 分类器的 recall 如何估计？已知 Prolog 场景中 strict classifier 存在严重 false negatives。
6. "Structural degeneration" 与 "component redundancy" 的边界在哪？ISR=0.5 的 formalization 是否一定是 "degenerate"？

---

## Missing References

1. **Shapley values (Lundberg & Lee, 2017)**：LOO 的自然泛化，可处理组件交互效应。讨论 LOO vs Shapley 的 trade-off（计算成本 2ⁿ vs n）可增强方法论讨论。[R1 提出, R2 AGREE]
2. **PRoSFI — Chen et al. (2026), arXiv:2603.29500**：将形式验证作为 process reward 引入 RL 训练，与 ISR-as-reward 实验直接相关。[R2 提出, R1 AGREE]
3. **"Pushing the Boundaries of Natural Reasoning: Interleaved Bonus from Formal-Logic Verification" (arXiv:2601.22642)**：将形式逻辑验证动态交织到生成过程中。[R2 提出, R1 AGREE]

注：R1 建议引用 Ng et al. (1999) potential-based shaping，R2 指出该引用已在 references.bib 和 related_work.tex Section 2 首句中出现（R1 事实性错误）。

## Visual Layout Check（非评分项）

状态：**done**（2 warnings, 6 info）

**Warnings:**
1. **Pages 5-6 连续纯文本**：§4.1-§4.3 实验叙述段无任何 figure/table/equation 穿插，审稿人易失去视觉锚点。建议将关键数字提炼为 inline 表格。
2. **Page 8 拥挤**：Figure 2 + Figure 3 + Table 2 挤在同一页，内部文字（axis label, legend）接近可读性下限（7-8pt）。与 pages 5-6 纯文本形成不均匀节奏。建议 Table 2 前移。

**Info:**
- Section 5 从第 8 页底延续至第 9 页上部约 1/4（与 SF9 页数溢出问题一致）
- Figure 1 单栏宽度，视觉冲击力有限
- Figure 2/3 被压缩到单栏宽度，tick label 和 legend 字号偏小
- Table 1 排版优秀（booktabs 风格、最优值加粗、caption 自包含）
- Algorithm 1 提供良好视觉结构
- Appendix Tables 7-10 数据密集但功能可接受

---

## Overall Assessment

**评分: 3.0 — Findings（可被 Findings 接收）**

**置信度: 4/5**（两位 Reviewer 均熟悉相关文献，仔细审读全文含附录）

**理由:**

论文填补了 neurosymbolic RL 中缺乏结构质量诊断的真实空白，ISR 揭示的 prove rate 与结构质量之间的隐性脱钩（214× ISR 崩塌 vs PR 上升）具有说服力，hacking taxonomy 有独立参考价值。实验设计在 case study 范围内严谨透明。

然而，以下因素限制了论文达到 Main Conference (4.0) 的水平：
- **统计支撑不足**：核心 Goodhart 声明（C2）的直接统计检验不显著（p=0.182），N=3 seeds 导致多项比较 underpowered
- **方法论新颖性有限**：LOO ablation 是 50 年老技术的新场景应用，真正贡献在实证发现
- **泛化范围窄**：仅 Qwen3-4B + FOLIO/ProofFOL，无成功的跨模型验证
- **Framing 不一致**：title "Case Study" 与 contributions 通用语言之间的张力

论文适合 EMNLP 2026 Findings：技术执行扎实，发现有价值，但 novelty 和 scope 不足以竞争 Main Conference。

**建议修改优先级:**
1. [高] MF1: 区分 Goodhart composition shift（显著）和 prevalence claim（不显著）
2. [高] MF2: 对关键比较标注 exploratory 或提供 power analysis
3. [中] SF9: 压缩正文至 8 页
4. [中] SF1: 统一 case study framing
5. [中] SF5: 提供 excluding-s43 sensitivity analysis
6. [中] SF6: 正文增加 premise-count-matched ISR 对比
7. [低] SF7/SF10/Minor issues

---

## Detailed Comments（逐节）

### Abstract
- "compositional Goodhart effect" 已较前版改善，但仍需在 p=0.182 旁注明 composition shift 有显著支撑而 prevalence reduction 无显著支撑
- "whose diagnostic value is realized through mode-level analysis" 表述好，准确限定了 ISR scalar 的作用

### Introduction
- 逻辑流清晰：blind spot → component necessity testing → diagnostic → Goodhart
- L28 "99.5% degradation" 已标注 seed~42，L29 "all 3 seeds: ≤7%" 提供 3-seed 证据，改善到位
- C2 "compositional Goodhart effect: TS amplification without significant prevalence reduction (p=0.182)" 措辞准确

### Method (Section 3)
- Algorithm 1 伪代码清晰简洁
- §3.2 premise-count confound 讨论（3.2×→1.06×）位置正确，但首次在此出现、Table 1 未反映形成呈现落差
- LOO independence assumption (§3.2 末段) 方向正确但缺少量化

### Experiments (Section 4)
- §4.1 Statistical considerations 段提供了必要的 N=3 framework
- §4.2 ISR as Diagnostic：collapse trajectory (214× for s42) 是最强证据
- §4.3 Goodhart Effects：最弱章节——核心比较 p=0.182 但叙述语气仍偏强
- §4.4 Cross-Domain：Prolog 结果（3/3 seeds 0% N-H）有力；ProofFOL 结果（p=0.10）标注 exploratory 恰当
- Table 1 设计好（双行 ISR 3/5-seed），但 ISR* 列 "1.06×‡" 在 5-seed 行无对应 matched 值

### Related Work (Section 2)
- 覆盖面广（neurosymbolic, process rewards, Goodhart, mutation testing）
- 与 Kim et al. (2026) 区分存在（inference-time vs training-time）但可更显著
- 缺少与 Helff et al. (2026) IPT 的方法论对比

### Discussion/Conclusion (Section 5)
- Observer/optimizer asymmetry 总结精准
- 实践建议有价值但缺少过渡
- 可能溢出 8 页限制

### Limitations (Section 6)
- 覆盖充分：single model, 3 seeds, ISR≠semantic faithfulness, data scale, temporal snapshot
- LLaMA-3.2-3B cold-start 作为 capability threshold 讨论得当

### Appendix
- 详尽且有组织：hyperparams, algorithm, extended discussion, hacking modes, per-seed results, O(1) ablation, LLaMA cold-start
- PG/TSb 发现和分类器验证增强了主文可信度
---

## ⚠️ 处理协议(强制,逐条执行,禁止简略)

**收到上面的审稿报告后必须按以下顺序处理**:

1. **逐条编号通读**: 把报告里所有问题、修改建议一项一项列出来(R1, R2, R3...),
   即使有的看起来重复或次要也要单独保留 — 不允许漏任何一条。

2. **每条给出详细修复方案**:
   - 补实验: 写明实验配置(baseline / 超参 / seed / dataset / metric) + 预估 GPU 时长
   - 改论文: 写明改哪一节、改写思路、新引用是否需要补
   - 改 framing / 措辞: 写明原文 + 改写后版本
   - 写 rebuttal: 写明论点和支撑证据
   - 拒绝: 写明拒绝理由(reviewer 误读 / 超出 scope / 已有反驳证据)

3. **必须调 `post_to_reviewer` 让 Research Reviewer 补充意见**:
   把完整审稿报告 + 你的逐条初步方案打包发给 Reviewer, 让 ta 独立审视有没有
   遗漏、方案可行性、技术细节是否合理。**这一步不可省** — Reviewer 是你的
   独立审查员, 他对每条审稿意见的判断和你互补。

4. **收到 Reviewer 补充后综合定稿**: 把 reviewer 反馈合并进你的 Action Matrix,
   再安排 Main Agent 执行(补实验 / 改论文)或向用户确认 venue 变更。

5. **同步 Issue Tracker**: Action Matrix 定稿后, 用 `read_local_file` 读
   `projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml`, 把本轮处理的 issue 标
   `in_progress`、Reviewer 已确认修复的标 `resolved`, 调 `update_review_issues`
   写回。这是跨轮次的修复追踪 — Continue 模式审稿要靠这个表识别复发问题。

**禁止**:
- 跳过第 3 步直接派 Main Agent
- 把多条审稿意见合并成一条 action
- 对低 priority 意见用"暂不处理"一笔带过而不给出明确理由
- 整体性"已知悉"回复

**MF1** [critical][framing] Goodhart composition shift (显著) vs prevalence reduction (不显著) 混合
→ 方案：在 abstract/intro/§4.3 中进一步拆分两个子发现。Abstract L6 改为明确两层："ISR exhibits a compositional Goodhart effect: it significantly amplifies TS at the expense of CE (CMH $p<10^{-10}$, sample-level) without significantly reducing overall hacking prevalence ($p=0.182$, seed-level underpowered)"。§4.3 opening 同步修改。纯文本。

**MF2** [critical][experiment] N=3 power analysis 缺失
→ 方案：在 §4.1 Statistical considerations 段末添加 power analysis 句："With $N{=}3$ seeds, seed-level tests achieve adequate power only for very large effects; comparisons not reaching significance (ISR vs.\ Random non-hacking $p{=}0.182$, ProofFOL $p{=}0.10$) are exploratory observations." 纯文本，不需补实验。

**SF1** [warning][framing] Case study 与 C1-C2 通用语言矛盾
→ 方案：C1 改 "STA as a diagnostic framework" → "STA applied as a diagnostic"；C2 开头加 "In this case study,"。统一限定语。纯文本。

**SF2** [warning][framing] LOO 方法论新颖性有限
→ 方案：已有 "novelty lies in application and findings" 声明。可在 Intro 进一步强化："our contribution is not the perturbation primitive but the empirical phenomena it surfaces"。已有类似表述，微调即可。

**SF3** [warning][experiment] 单模型限制
→ 方案：已在 title/limitations/intro 全面限定。不需新 action。标注 wont_fix (已充分缓解)。

**SF4** [warning][experiment] 乘法耦合 reward magnitude 混淆
→ 方案：§4.3 Multiplicative coupling 段已有讨论 + shuffled-ISR 控制。可加一句："The shuffled-ISR control uses the identical multiplicative form $r_{\text{exec}}\cdot(1+\beta\,\text{ISR}_{\text{shuffled}})$, isolating semantic correspondence from reward magnitude."。纯文本。

**SF5** [warning][experiment] Seed 43 excluding sensitivity analysis
→ 方案：需要 Agent 从现有 per-seed data 计算 excluding-s43 的 Table 1 所有 pooled 指标（PR, ISR*, TS%, CE%, N-H%），加入 Appendix 作为 sensitivity table。计算任务，无需 GPU。

**SF6** [warning][method] Premise-count matched ISR 正文呈现不足

**SF7** [warning][method] Helff et al. (2026) IPT 对比
→ 方案：Related Work §Reward hacking 段加 2-3 句："Helff et al. (2026) introduce Isomorphic Perturbation Testing (IPT), which verifies answers via semantically equivalent reformulations; STA operates at a different granularity (component-level LOO ablation on solver inputs vs. problem-level perturbation on natural language), targeting structural necessity rather than answer robustness." 需确认 Helff et al. 是否已在 bib 中。

**SF8** [warning][experiment] ProofFOL 分类器不对称标注
→ 方案：§4.4 ProofFOL ISR 段 L114 已有 "unlike FOLIO's eight-mode residual; the rates are therefore not directly comparable"。再在 Table 1 caption 或 ProofFOL mention 处加 "(upper bound; 2-mode classifier)" 标注。

**SF9** [warning][format] 正文溢出 8 页
→ 方案：Section 5 溢出约 15 行到第 9 页。需要压缩正文。主要策略：(a) 压缩 §4.3 Bimodal seed-dependence 段（3→2 句），(b) 压缩 §4.4 一些冗余叙述，(c) 收紧 Introduction 中已有重复的 seed 细节。目标回收 ~15 行。纯文本编辑。

**SF10** [warning][writing] 缩写密度
→ 方案：历史遗留，已多次处理。可在 §4.1 Setup 段末加一句 "We summarize notation in Appendix~\ref{app:notation}"，在 Appendix 加简表。低优先级。

        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_3/projects/solver-trace-ablation/docs/paper/introduction.tex",
        "content": "\\section{Introduction}\n\\label{sec:introduction}\n\nA GRPO-trained formalizer achieves a 93\\% prove rate on first-order logic problems, yet 87\\% of its proved outputs embed the conclusion verbatim among the premises.\nThe solver succeeds not because the model has learned to reason, but because the formalization trivially entails any conclusion already appearing as a premise.\nThis structural shortcut (an instance of shortcut learning~\\citep{geirhos2020shortcut} in the neurosymbolic setting) is invisible to execution-based evaluation: prove rate registers success regardless of whether the proof reflects genuine derivation or degenerate circular embedding.\n\n\\paragraph{The execution-only blind spot.}\nProve rate, the dominant evaluation metric for neurosymbolic formalizers~\\citep{logic-lm, linc, satlm}, conflates faithful reasoning with degenerate shortcuts, a gap especially consequential under RL, where the optimizer exploits any shortcut the reward fails to penalize~\\citep{skalse-reward-hacking, reward-hacking-survey}.\nRecent work applies GRPO~\\citep{deepseekmath} to train LLMs that generate solver-verified formal programs~\\citep{mellgren-prolog-grpo, logic-rl} using binary execution feedback; this drives rapid PR gains but provides no signal on component necessity.\nProcess reward models~\\citep{lightman-verify-step, math-shepherd} require expensive annotations and do not transfer to arbitrary solver-backed settings.\nA separate line of work documents that LLMs already game logical formalization at inference time~\\citep{kim-game-formalization, bondarenko-spec-gaming}; what is missing is an annotation-free \\emph{diagnostic} for component necessity in neurosymbolic outputs.\n\n\\paragraph{Component necessity testing.}\nWe address this gap with \\emph{Solver Trace Ablation} (STA), a diagnostic applying \\emph{component necessity testing}, leave-one-out (LOO) ablation on solver inputs, to quantify component necessity.\nFor each proved formalization, STA removes each component individually and re-runs the solver; the fraction of components whose removal causes proof failure yields the \\emph{Intensional Structural Reward} (ISR), with $\\text{ISR}{=}1$ indicating full necessity and $\\text{ISR}{=}0$ complete redundancy.\nLOO ablation itself is standard~\\citep{demillo1978, jia-harman-survey}; our contribution is the empirical discovery that the same LOO structure making ISR informative as a diagnostic also makes it vulnerable to gaming as a reward, a compositional Goodhart effect on structural metrics.\nISR captures component necessity rather than semantic faithfulness (downstream NLI: $\\rho{=}{-}0.049$, $p{=}0.187$).\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=0.95\\columnwidth]{figures/fig5_training_isr_curves.pdf}\n\\caption{ISR training trajectories across reward conditions (proved samples only, rolling $w{=}20$). Exec-only collapses to $\\text{ISR}{\\approx}0$, confirming structural degeneration (\\S\\ref{sec:diagnostic}); the ${\\sim}0.5$ plateau is an $n{=}2$ artifact. ISR $\\beta{=}0.1$ seeds rise toward $\\text{ISR}{\\approx}1$ via TS gaming (Goodhart effect, \\S\\ref{sec:goodhart}); random remains low.}\n\\label{fig:training-isr-curves}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\paragraph{ISR as diagnostic.}\nAs a post-hoc diagnostic on a GRPO formalizer trained with FOLIO~\\citep{folio}, ISR reveals a structural collapse trajectory invisible to PR (Figure~\\ref{fig:training-isr-curves}): for seed~42, $\\text{ISR}^{*}{:}\\,0.642\\to 0.132\\to 0.003$ over 100--1000 steps (99.5\\% degradation; all 3 seeds: ${\\leq}7\\%$ non-hacking by 1000 steps) while PR rises $32\\%{\\to}96\\%$.\nThree seeds diverge radically (PR $\\sigma{=}47$\\,pp), each converging to a qualitatively different mode (conclusion embedding, repetition collapse, and tautological collapse), yet all reach ${\\geq}93\\%$ structural hacking by 1000 steps.\nAn SFT baseline on identical data reaches $\\text{PR}{=}13.0\\%$ with 92.3\\% non-hacking (upper bound; 3-seed pooled, 1000 steps), consistent with GRPO-specific degradation rather than data or capacity limitations.\n\n\\paragraph{Goodhart effects.}\nRepurposed as a training signal, $r = r_{\\text{exec}} \\cdot (1 + \\beta\\,\\text{ISR})$, ISR produces a compositional Goodhart effect~\\citep{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy, karwowski-goodhart-rl, gao-reward-overoptimization}.\nOn FOLIO, this instantiates \\emph{regressional} Goodhart~\\citep{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy}: $P{\\to}P$ tautologies yield $\\text{ISR}{\\geq}0.5$, so ISR rewards the shortcut; at $\\beta{=}0.1$, ISR increases TS rates in 3 of 5 seeds (70.6\\% vs.\\ 13.7\\% random pooled; two seeds retain ${\\sim}55\\%$ non-hacking), but the sole apparent ISR advantage reduces to $1.06\\times$ after controlling for premise count.\nAt $\\beta{\\geq}0.2$, \\emph{extremal} Goodhart dominates ($\\text{PR}{=}66{\\pm}42\\%$ ISR, $54{\\pm}35\\%$ random at $\\beta{=}0.3$).\nOn ProofFOL, hacking yields $\\text{ISR}{=}0.0$, so ISR correctly penalizes shortcuts, associated with beneficial instability (76.8\\% vs.\\ 17.4\\% genuine under a coarser classifier, $p{=}0.10$).\nISR's asymmetry is the central finding: \\emph{reliable as observer, dataset-dependent as optimizer.}\n\n\\paragraph{Contributions.}\nIn this case study (Qwen3-4B, 6 reward conditions, 3--5 seeds, 2 logic types, 2 dataset families, up to 1000 steps), we contribute: (\\textbf{C1}) STA as a diagnostic framework (ISR metric, eight-mode hacking taxonomy, and cross-domain evaluation protocol) revealing structural collapse invisible to PR (${\\leq}7\\%$ non-hacking, 3 seeds, 1000 steps; 99.5\\% in seed~42), with an SFT control consistent with GRPO-specific degradation (\\S\\ref{sec:diagnostic}); (\\textbf{C2}) a compositional Goodhart effect on ISR-as-reward: TS amplification without significant prevalence reduction ($p{=}0.182$; apparent ISR advantage collapses to $1.06\\times$ after deconfounding; shuffled-ISR control rules out distributional shape; \\S\\ref{sec:goodhart}), with cross-domain evidence: confirmed in Prolog (3/3 seeds 0\\% non-hacking) and exploratory in ProofFOL ($p{=}0.10$), showing dataset-dependent outcomes (\\S\\ref{sec:crossdomain}).\nThese are cautionary results from a Qwen3-4B case study, replicated across dataset families, relevant to any work treating a structural proxy as a training signal (generalization scope discussed in \\S\\ref{sec:limitations}).\n",
        "numLines": 42,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 42
      }
    },

{
  "parentUuid": "5d9e5197-7eed-4615-b73a-6e469c13de67",
  "isSidechain": false,
  "attachment": {
    "type": "file",
    "filename": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_3/projects/solver-trace-ablation/docs/paper/method.tex",
    "content": {
      "type": "text",
      "file": {
        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_3/projects/solver-trace-ablation/docs/paper/method.tex",
        "content": "\\section{Method}\n\\label{sec:method}\n\n\\subsection{Task and GRPO Training}\n\\label{sec:task}\n\nGiven premises $P_1,\\ldots,P_k$ and conclusion $C$, the task is to produce a formal program $F=\\{c_1,\\ldots,c_n\\}$ verified by an external solver $S\\in\\{\\text{Prover9, SWI-Prolog}\\}$.\nWe train with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)~\\citep{deepseekmath} ($K{=}4$), where each rollout receives binary execution reward $r_{\\text{exec}}(x) = \\mathbf{1}[S(F_x){=}\\textit{proved}]$.\nThis optimizes prove rate but provides no signal about \\emph{component necessity}: a formalization embedding the conclusion among its premises receives the same reward as a faithful derivation.\nAll experiments draw from FOLIO~\\citep{folio}.\n\n\\subsection{Solver Trace Ablation (STA)}\n\\label{sec:sta}\n\nSTA tests whether each component of a proved formalization is individually necessary for proof success (Algorithm~\\ref{alg:sta}).\nGiven a proved $x$ with $n$ components, STA constructs $n$ ablated variants $x\\setminus\\{c_i\\}$ and re-runs the solver (Algorithm~\\ref{alg:sta}).\nA component is \\emph{necessary} if its removal causes proof failure.\nWhat constitutes a component depends on the logic: premises in FOL, clauses or ground facts in Prolog.\nLOO ablation itself is standard in mutation testing and sensitivity analysis~\\citep{demillo1978, jia-harman-survey}; novelty lies in its application as a GRPO-hacking diagnostic and the empirical phenomena it surfaces.\n\n\\begin{algorithm}[t]\n\\caption{Solver Trace Ablation (STA)}\n\\label{alg:sta}\n\\begin{algorithmic}[1]\n\\REQUIRE Proved formalization $x = \\{c_1, \\ldots, c_n\\}$, solver $S$\n\\ENSURE $\\text{ISR}(x) \\in [0, 1]$\n\\STATE $\\textit{necessary} \\gets 0$\n\\FOR{$i = 1$ to $n$}\n    \\IF{$S(x \\setminus \\{c_i\\}) = \\textit{failed}$}\n        \\STATE $\\textit{necessary} \\gets \\textit{necessary} + 1$\n    \\ENDIF\n\\ENDFOR\n\\RETURN $\\textit{necessary} \\,/\\, n$\n\\end{algorithmic}\n\\end{algorithm}\n\n\\paragraph{Intensional Structural Reward.}\\footnote{Not \\emph{intentional}; \\emph{intensional} contrasts with extensional (execution-only) evaluation.}\nISR is the fraction of necessary components:\n\\begin{equation}\n  \\text{ISR}(x) = \\frac{1}{n}\\sum_{i=1}^{n}\\mathbf{1}\\bigl[S(x\\setminus\\{c_i\\})=\\textit{failed}\\bigr]\n  \\label{eq:isr}\n\\end{equation}\nISR is defined only for proved formalizations: ISR${=}1$ means every component is necessary; ISR${=}0$ means complete redundancy.\nISR quantifies component redundancy, not semantic faithfulness; its diagnostic value is realized through the downstream mode breakdown (\\S\\ref{sec:taxonomy}), not the scalar alone.\nWe report \\emph{proved-only} ISR (denoted ISR$^*$) throughout to avoid conflating component necessity with prove rate.\n\n\\paragraph{Limitations of ISR.}\nISR captures component necessity under LOO removal ($\\rho{=}{-}0.049$ with NLI accuracy; Appendix~\\ref{app:extended-discussion}).\nISR is mechanically coupled to component count ($n{=}2 \\Rightarrow \\text{ISR}{\\geq}0.5$); premise-count-matched analysis reduces the raw ISR advantage from $3.2\\times$ to $1.06\\times$ (\\S\\ref{sec:goodhart}).\nIn particular, TS ($P{\\to}P$) achieves $\\text{ISR}{\\geq}0.5$ by construction since the sole premise is necessary for the trivial proof; we therefore combine ISR with text-match classifiers for complete hacking detection (\\S\\ref{sec:taxonomy-method}; $O(1)$ binary variant in Appendix~\\ref{app:o1-ablation}).\n\n\\paragraph{LOO independence assumption.}\nLOO ablation tests individual, not joint, component necessity~\\citep{jia-harman-survey}, systematically underestimating ISR when jointly-necessary components exist; the observed collapse is therefore conservative.\n\n\\subsection{Reward Conditions and Hacking Modes}\n\\label{sec:rewards}\n\\label{sec:taxonomy-method}\n\nWe study six conditions: exec-only ($r_{\\text{exec}}$), ISR low/high-dose ($r_{\\text{exec}}\\cdot(1{+}\\beta\\,\\text{ISR})$ at $\\beta\\in\\{0.1,0.3\\}$), matched random controls ($r_{\\text{exec}}\\cdot(1{+}\\beta u)$, $u\\sim\\mathcal{U}(0,1)$), and a supervised fine-tuning (SFT) baseline (negative log-likelihood on gold formalizations).\nMultiplicative bonuses apply only to proved samples, keeping the prove-rate floor intact.\nRandom isolates ISR-specific signal from generic reward-variance effects; SFT isolates whether structural degeneration arises from RL optimization or data exposure alone.\nEach RL condition uses seeds $\\{42,43,44\\}$ and identical training data.\nAn earlier convex formulation ($\\alpha r_{\\text{exec}} + \\beta r_{\\text{exec}}\\text{ISR}$, $\\alpha{+}\\beta{=}1$) was discarded because $\\text{ISR}{<}1$ always penalized relative to exec-only, collapsing PR (Appendix~\\ref{app:setup}).\n\n\\paragraph{Hacking taxonomy.}\nAcross conditions, seeds, and logic types we identify eight structural-degeneration modes (full definitions in Appendix~\\ref{app:hacking-modes}):\n\\textbf{CE} (conclusion embedded among multiple premises),\n\\textbf{TS} (single-premise conclusion duplication, subtypes TSa standard and TSb obfuscated tautology; targets the ISR signal directly, since removing the sole premise forces $\\text{ISR}\\geq 0.5$),\n\\textbf{PG} (premise-subset gaming: each component is LOO-necessary but the premise set is a strict subset of what the problem requires, inflating ISR without faithful reasoning; Appendix~\\ref{app:h8}),\n\\textbf{tautological collapse} (minimal two-component $P{\\to}P$ without explicit ISR inflation),\n\\textbf{repetition collapse} (identical clauses repeated to inflate component count),\n\\textbf{complexity-without-utility} (elaborate but vacuous premises),\n\\textbf{contradiction explosion} (contradictory premises trivializing any conclusion via \\emph{ex falso}), and\n\\textbf{query-as-fact gaming} (Prolog-specific: emit the NL question as an atom asserted as both fact and query; evades text-match classifiers).\nModes are identified by a two-stage procedure: strict normalized text matching (CE/TS) plus functional analysis of the per-sample $n_{\\text{necessary}}$ distribution (catches modes evading text matching).\nWe report both classifications in \\S\\ref{sec:experiments}.\n\nLow ISR signals redundancy under exec-only but metric gaming under ISR-as-reward; the mode breakdown, not the ISR scalar, licenses interpretation.\n",
        "numLines": 79,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 79
      }
    },
    "displayPath": "../../../docs/paper/method.tex"
  },

        "content": "\\begin{abstract}\nWe study structural degeneration in neurosymbolic reasoning trained with GRPO: models achieve high prove rates while most outputs embed the conclusion among premises---a degenerate shortcut invisible to execution reward.\nWe introduce \\emph{Solver Trace Ablation} (STA), a diagnostic applying leave-one-out necessity testing to solver inputs; the fraction of necessary components yields the \\emph{Intensional Structural Reward} (ISR), whose diagnostic value is realized through mode-level analysis.\nAs a diagnostic, component-necessity testing reveals a collapse trajectory hidden from prove rate: ISR falls to near-zero over training while prove rate rises, with each seed converging to a qualitatively different hacking mode.\nA supervised fine-tuning baseline corroborates that the degradation is GRPO-specific.\nWhen repurposed as a training reward, ISR exhibits a compositional Goodhart effect: models shift from conclusion embedding to tautological shortcutting without reducing overall hacking prevalence ($p{=}0.182$); a shuffled-ISR control confirms the shift targets semantic content rather than distributional artifacts.\nCross-logic (Prolog) and cross-dataset (ProofFOL) evidence confirms structural degeneration generalizes, documenting ISR's core asymmetry: reliable as a diagnostic, dataset-dependent as an optimization target.\\footnote{Code and data: \\url{[anonymized]}}\n\\end{abstract}\n",
        "numLines": 9,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 9
      }
    },

        "content": "\\section{Discussion and Conclusion}\n\\label{sec:discussion}\n\nSTA exposes structural degeneration invisible to execution-based evaluation: ISR reveals near-complete collapse under exec-only GRPO (e.g., $214\\times$ for seed~42) that PR hides, while SFT results are consistent with GRPO-specific degradation (\\S\\ref{sec:diagnostic}).\nCross-logic replication in Prolog confirms the phenomenon generalizes beyond FOL (\\S\\ref{sec:crosslogic}).\nRepurposed as a reward, ISR produces a compositional Goodhart effect: on FOLIO, it shifts hacking from CE to TS in 3 of 5 seeds (supplementary CMH $p{<}10^{-15}$; two seeds retain ${\\sim}55\\%$ non-hacking) without significantly reducing overall hacking prevalence ($p{=}0.182$); on ProofFOL, beneficial instability yields 76.8\\% vs.\\ 17.4\\% non-hacking (upper bound; \\S\\ref{sec:setup}) ($p{=}0.10$).\nDeconfounding reduces the sole apparent ISR reward advantage to $1.06\\times$; a shuffled-ISR control confirms that semantic content, not distributional shape, drives the compositional shift (\\S\\ref{sec:goodhart}).\nThe central finding is ISR's observer/optimizer asymmetry: the same LOO structure that makes ISR informative as a diagnostic makes it gameable as a reward, with outcomes depending on whether the dataset's hacking geometry satisfies the metric.\n\nFor practitioners in settings similar to ours (medium-scale models with LOO-tractable formalization; \\S\\ref{sec:limitations}): never evaluate neurosymbolic systems by prove rate alone (use ISR and component count as joint diagnostics with mode-level breakdown), always report multi-seed results, and use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only since any dose induces gaming or collapse when optimized (Appendix~\\ref{app:practical-guidelines}).\n",

{
  "text": "【R23 审稿报告独立审视请求】\n\nR23 Fresh Review 给出 Overall 3.0 (Findings)，相比 R22 的 3.0-3.5 略降。请独立审视以下审稿报告摘要和我的逐条行动方案，重点关注：(1) 方案可行性 (2) 是否有遗漏 (3) 优先级是否合理 (4) 页面空间约束下的取舍建议。\n\n## R23 关键问题摘要\n\n### MF1: Goodhart effect 表述仍混淆显著/不显著\n审稿认为\"compositional Goodhart effect\"标签仍混合了两个不同显著性水平的子发现：composition shift (CMH p<10⁻¹⁵ 显著) vs prevalence reduction (p=0.182 不显著)。需要在 abstract/intro/§4.3/conclusion 中明确拆分。\n\n### MF2: N=3 seeds 统计检验力不足\n审稿要求：(a) 添加 power analysis 说明 N=3 下能检测到的最小效应量；(b) 将非显著比较标记为 \"exploratory\"。\n\n### SF1: C1/C2 贡献表述缺 case-study 限定\n### SF5: 排除 s43 后的完整敏感性分析表（目前只有叙述，缺完整数据）\n### SF6: Premise-count-matched ISR 应更突出（进入 Table 1 或正文核心位置）\n### SF7: 缺 Helff et al. (2026) IPT 方法比较\n### SF9: 主正文仍超 8 页（估计超约 15 行）\n### SF2-SF4, SF8, SF10: 较小的文本修订\n\n## 我的逐条行动方案\n\n**MF1** [HIGH]: 在 abstract L6、intro L33-34、§4.3 开头、conclusion 中将 Goodhart 发现拆分为两个明确子句：\n- Sub-finding A: composition shift CE→TS (CMH p<10⁻¹⁵, significant)\n- Sub-finding B: overall prevalence unchanged (p=0.182, n.s.)\n保留 \"compositional Goodhart effect\" 术语但让两个子发现各自带 p 值。\n\n**MF2** [HIGH]: 在 §4.1 添加 1 句 power analysis：\"With $N{=}3$ seeds per condition, seed-level tests are powered to detect only large effects ($d{\\geq}2.0$); non-significant comparisons (e.g., prevalence $p{=}0.182$) are therefore exploratory.\"\n\n**SF1** [MEDIUM]: 在 C1/C2 中加 \"In this case study\" 或 \"within our setting\" 限定词。\n\n**SF5** [MEDIUM]: 让 Agent 从实验数据计算排除 s43 后的 Table 1 全部 pooled stats，放入 Appendix 表格。正文加一句引用。\n\n**SF6** [MEDIUM]: 在 Table 1 添加 matched-ISR 行，或在 §4.3 正文中将 \"3.2× → 1.06×\" 结果提升到更显眼位置。\n\n**SF7** [MEDIUM]: Related Work 添加 2-3 句 Helff et al. IPT 比较。\n\n**SF9** [HIGH]: 需要从主正文压缩约 15 行。计划：\n- intro 贡献段 C1/C2 压缩（~3 行）\n- §4.1 合并重复表述（~3 行）\n- §4.3 压缩 Goodhart 机制解释（~3 行）\n- §4.4 cross-domain 压缩（~3 行）\n- conclusion 压缩（~3 行）\n\n**SF2** [LOW]: §4.1 第一段加 \"case study\" 限定。\n**SF3** [LOW]: 已有 caveats，保持现状或微调。\n**SF4** [LOW]: 100-step 比较已标 \"supplementary evidence\"，保持。\n**SF8** [LOW]: ProofFOL classifier 标签改为 \"coarser binary classifier\"。\n**SF10** [LOW]: Appendix 缩写表——如有空间加，否则 wont_fix。\n\n**Missing References**: helff2026ipt — 需要确认真实性后添加到 bib。\n\n## 请 Reviewer 重点审视\n\n1.
